## NET-M3 — real IP HTTPS without browser SNI (2026-10-17)

- **Rootless exact-head forensic result:** [#1966](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/pull/1966) at `360c08eafd3008a8fa3a120f76b8e6758e8fcf6e` [CI #39030505128](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/39030505128) still failed `server-rootless-e2e`. With the dedicated ephemeral `XD_HTTPS_BIND=192.168.254.246` publishing correctly, direct HTTPS curl returned OpenSSL `tlsv1 alert internal error` even though Caddy logged successful issuance of the exact private IP certificate. Loopback-routed synthetic diagnostic was unsurprisingly unable to connect because host 8443 bound only on the alias. Root cause candidate: browsers/curl connecting via IP normally omit SNI; pinned Caddy 2.10.2 has a documented missing-TLS-policy/default-SNI adaptation issue (upstream https://github.com/caddyserver/caddy/issues/7325, fixed only after 2.10.2). No evidence supports bypassing TLS.
- **Production fix under test:** In BOTH static LAN-only and LAN+domain Caddyfiles, set `default_sni {$XD_LAN_HTTPS_IP}` and use explicit `https://{$XD_LAN_HTTPS_IP}` site scheme so Caddy's adapted server emits `tls_connection_policies` with a private-IP fallback identity. Real pinned Caddy image build must inspect BOTH adapted JSONs and require the exact trusted default SNI, not just a parse. Preserve `tls internal`, valid IP SAN, current dual AliDNS, no new external port, no plaintext WAN/LAN password login, no global insecure workaround. Rootless end-to-end keeps genuine direct-IP `curl --cacert` and must prove actual private-IP TLS chain, SAN and readiness before install completes.
- **Gate:** Recreate exactly ONE M3 work commit on frozen `master@02464a633d86afe6fc7bf00cae399e36f459bb3a`, preserve unrelated master and previous M3 files, require a NEW complete 76-job exact-head CI incl. server-rootless-e2e and final-gate. Until full green, #1966 Draft; downstream #1967 M4 must be reconstructed on this exact work HEAD, remains Draft and `apply_supported=false`. Operator device trust/root fingerprint, home DMZ firewall and true Web/desktop login not physically tested. No direct Caddy Host Apply/Stop.

## NET-M2 — explicit opt-in same-port LAN IP HTTPS login behind Caddy private CA (2026-10-17)

- **New exact-head CI failure and bounded correction:** `bedfda2ba8ffe9f7b85e88502d2906d7c15c2fb9` triggered [full CI #39006872254](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/39006872254). The `caddy-image` Dockerfile parse failed because one new `RUN` used **two** literal continuation backslashes, so `caddy adapt` appeared as an unknown Dockerfile instruction; meanwhile Desktop's one remaining failed Node assertion searched for literal `\\u0024` instead of the actual Compose `${XD_LAN_HTTPS_IP:-}` substitution. Correct just those source-contract mistakes, make the copied Caddy selector script executable with `chmod 0755` in the image (otherwise production entrypoint would fail despite a green Caddy parser), and add a targeted execute-bit regression. Rebuild EXACTLY ONE scoped PR commit on unchanged frozen `master@32b7baaad3e63139026c611ba46390be4d38318c`, force-with-lease only the work branch, and demand a NEW accurate-HEAD complete 76-job CI incl. caddy-image, desktop-tests and final-gate; do not merge on the failed prior run. Independent Server IP default-domainless, dual enable/disable and controlled apply are NOT implemented by this opt-in PR; keep separate due to DMZ/public cert trust.
- **User scope expansion before NET-M1 delivery:** Direct NET-M1 [#1958](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/pull/1958) already has Web/Mobile Web/PWA login/avatar manual URL entry and Desktop Agent existing logout/edit-server flow but initially did NOT add any working Server IP listener. User explicitly requires **Server must also support IP login**, not a dead UI link. Keep this same PR and ONE scoped NET-M1+M2 work commit on immutable `master@32b7baaad3e63139026c611ba46390be4d38318c`, not two conflicting branches or an unsafe late incremental commit. Prior UI-only CI does not validate new Caddy/installer bytes. Don't change unrelated Admin Caddy restricted apply or Places/CAS/Files/PWA/LocalPush/perf/race work.
- **Precise deployment behavior:** Opt-in `XD_LAN_HTTPS_IP=192.168.2.4` (any canonical RFC1918 address) only when `XD_DOMAIN` is present; installer and runtime Caddy script each independently validate and reject invalid, non-private, octal, Unicode, credential-bearing or ambiguous inputs. Caddy selects a separate *static* `Caddyfile.lan-ip` containing existing public AliDNS domain site and a private-IP `tls internal` virtual host; both use the existing 443 container / `XD_HTTPS_PORT` host listener and same Web/API. Default option absent exactly keeps existing Caddyfile/http mode, `XD_WEB_BIND=127.0.0.1` for domain mode and no new HTTP login listener. Keep rootless/backup/upgrade transaction and Caddy Rescue safety contracts unchanged; `XD_ALLOWED_ORIGIN` need not widen because browser IP/login API is same-origin.
- **TLS and modem DMZ:** User's UNR030H1 DMZ points to Linux `192.168.2.4`. Binding alone does not keep a port private from WAN. No new open host port, no `0.0.0.0:3000`, no public CA for RFC1918, no skipping certificate errors. `tls internal` root is in persisted Caddy data and each browser/client must independently and securely trust/fingerprint-check the local CA before IP HTTPS login works. Refuse to auto-install CA or export via HTTP. Existing domain TLS continues DNS-01. Treat IP and domain as separate Origin/auth/Service Worker without sharing Refresh Tokens or passwords. Restore prior mode by clearing opt-in and controlled Caddy redeploy, not Live Caddy Apply/Stop controls.
- **Test/evidence discipline:** New POSIX selector unit contract and negative cases, true image `caddy adapt` validation of public-domain+IP Caddyfile, installer static strict-gate tests, existing browser/desktop security/transfer suite, full accurate-head 76-job GitHub CI incl. Go API PostgreSQL Race, Rootless, Web/Desktop/Linux/Windows, artifacts/images, single-commit/final-gate. Stay Draft until complete green and clean mergeability, then Ready/Rebase and branch cleanup. No physical modem/DNS/Linux deployment, actual CA trust installation, off-host TLS, NAT, or end-to-end customer login outcome is claimed prior to separate physical checks. ## P0-Places-A3d2m1b2b2c2 — private PostgreSQL retry cooldown and terminal Geoconv receipt (2026-10-17)

- **Reconciled master and scoped delivery:** GitHub `lazyxu/xdrive`; #1941 exact one-work HEAD `b300b29b7bd3974697a2b92bc7535e0f71d5321c` passed full accurate [76-job CI #38996634700](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/38996634700) (25 success/51 conditional skips incl. real PostgreSQL Go API Race, Go Linux/Windows, Web/Desktop, Rootless, artifacts, single-commit and final-gate), Ready/Rebase merged as `master@56f9114cd950e60be6bc06a182de36d7729d9ce5`; #1941 and #1930 closed and direct Places/Geoconv Open PR/branch searches empty. After unrelated master work, freeze `master@faf709d3fdec652b515104991b5ff144b71f7dab`, ONE scoped commit on `feat/gallery-places-a3-d2m1b2b2c2-durable-retry-20261017`. Leave unrelated Files/PWA/UI stability, CAS/performance, Admin/Devices, Race and Local Push untouched.
- **Durability gap and implementation:** The existing explicit one-photo map-index job allowed at most 3 claims but had NO durable failed terminal state: an expired third lease could remain indefinitely `leased` and the Viewer could not explain what happened. Add private `failed` terminal receipt and nullable PostgreSQL `retry_after`; after an actual claimed, unsuccessfully completed conversion, settle the exact Owner/Asset/source/consent/lease token/attempt under the same locked transaction order, with a separate 2-second cleanup Context if the request Context has been canceled. First retry cools for 15s, second for 60s on PostgreSQL clock, third settles permanently to `failed`; clear live lease token/expiry atomically, never persist provider errors, AK, GPS, URLs or filenames in the job. An expired/crashed lease is repaired on the next **explicit** trusted Owner+Asset claim attempt ONLY, with cooldown (or terminal failure after three); never immediately claim in that same transaction. PostgreSQL-clock `retry_after` blocks early claims even across replicas. A renewed consent revision is required to requeue a failed job; replaying the old consent cannot retry.
- **Safety & acceptance:** Idempotent and source-bound User cancellation or consent revocation must win against cleanup, expired token, stale lease and failed historical attempts; successfully completed positions are never undone. Existing real PostgreSQL two-pool tests now cover expired-lease repair/retry after DB clock, max-3 terminal state, disabled immediate reclaim, failed converter retry receipt and no stale GPS/AK egress. Shared MUI Viewer and shared models recognize `failed`, no platform-specific controller. No Server worker, scheduler, Browser/Server AK credential activation, upstream Baidu egress, new REST/Agent IPC, JSAPI Proxy/Loader/Cookie, BD09 inverse conversion or unbounded automatic 100k backfill. `runtime_enabled=false` and JSAPI4 children approved count 0; Baidu sole Provider, full-screen xDrive App Frame, 52px title bar, one common Web/Mobile Web/PWA/Desktop Gallery Server Range/VirtualCollection/Viewer and literal iOS 27 Photos 1:1 acceptance are unchanged.

## Continuation progress reporting

For every follow-up request to "continue" an ongoing development, audit, or CI/PR cleanup initiative, **begin the user-visible response with a concise state report before changing code or PRs**:

1. State the exact repository/provider, fixed master or work-branch baseline, and **current phase** (for example L01-A).
2. List **completed work** with concrete commit/PR/CI/merge evidence; distinguish implementation, test execution, green CI, and merged delivery. Never label planned or merely reviewed code as complete.
3. List **remaining gaps**, blockers, and the next bounded, testable step. Preserve named constraints from previous turns.
4. Inspect only materially related existing branches and PRs first. Prefer a viable unfinished branch; close superseded/abandoned related PRs and delete merged or proven-obsolete related branches only after checking their diffs and merge state. Do not clean unrelated work.
5. End each implementation turn with the updated phase, evidence-based done/not-done summary, test/CI status, and branch/PR disposition. Repeat this handoff at the beginning of the next continuation.

The progress report is required even when no code changes are possible. Follow the provider-specific GitHub/GitLab delivery and single-work-commit rules below; do not claim a GitLab push or cleanup if it was not actually performed.

## P1-PH1b3a2b2a — PostgreSQL shared/exclusive admission-fence primitive (2026-10-15)

- Following merged P1-PH1b3a2b1, advance the Photo Intelligence safe-drain contract **one bounded step**, without inventing a safe-stop UI. Add the fixed PostgreSQL `xd_photo_host_admission_gates` model (`name`, positive revision, `frozen`, updated-at) to explicit Server migrations, plus an unexported, fixed-key PostgreSQL **session-level shared advisory lock** admission helper. Always take the lock and read the matching row on the **same `sql.Conn`**, to prevent a TOCTOU window and avoid requiring an extra connection just for the decision read.
- The key `photo-host:admission:v1` MUST hash exactly like existing `sourceaccount.TryAcquire` so that a future exclusive maintenance writer using that fixed key cannot overtake ANY held shared admission lease. Shared locks must permit distinct concurrent read holders. Only after a future audited writer has acquired the corresponding EXCLUSIVE advisory lock and persisted a revisioned `frozen` row may a real global freeze barrier be claimed. `frozen=true` or an exclusive writer = no new admission from this helper; `frozen=false` with valid revision or an absent row in a **successfully migrated table** = admission allowed; missing table, DB/timeout error or invalid revision = fail closed. Close must release the same-session shared lock, including on any failed row read; heartbeat must prove connection responsiveness.
- **This is an internal, tested primitive only**: DO NOT attach it to running Face/Smart/OCR/Semantic/Person/Creative workers or expose an administrator Freeze/Unfreeze action until a separate P1-PH1b3a2b2b stage wires EVERY model-dependent work entry point, proves no pool starvation and retry/cancel correctness, and makes a revision-fenced, audited server-side writer. No stop/restart/Docker/Host commands, no new REST/IPC/MUI toggle, no persistent task payload/owner metadata, no Source, no positive fleet drain/approval. Existing 7-part manual Host checks, `effect-proof=unknown`, 4 model `activation_state=deployment-only` and enable-only Host Manager remain unchanged.
- Test with a real isolated PostgreSQL schema: missing migration fails closed, an empty migrated table remains normally admitting, 1-connection read works, concurrent shared leases coexist, future writer exclusive lock cannot overtake either, exclusive writer prevents new admission, positive frozen revision blocks, unfrozen revision admits, corrupt revision and cancellation fail closed, heartbeat and idempotent cleanup. Do not claim the runtime scheduler is already fenced. Require exact-head full CI and final-gate + one-work-commit PR before merge. Next P1-PH1b3a2b2b wires the shared lease to all required job paths and handles defer/retry semantics; subsequent P1-PH1b3a2b2c adds audited operator membership revision, frozen admission and per-instance ACK; only P1-PH1b3b may explore reversible safe stop. See [docs/service-dependencies.md](docs/service-dependencies.md#p1-ph1b3a2b2a-2026-10-15-admission-fence-core-without-freeze-authority).

## Navigation visual stability specialization (Mobile Web / Web / PWA / Desktop)

**Permanent no-jitter contract / P78 — MANDATORY for every future UI PR.** Read [docs/mobile-navigation-stability.md](docs/mobile-navigation-stability.md) BEFORE changing Files, Gallery, album folders, Viewer, Source, Search/Trash, Sidebar, settings, app navigation, deep links, pagination, or asynchronous loading. A correct-looking final screenshot is NOT acceptance. These rules apply to new pages, refactors and all four clients; page jitter is a blocking regression, not optional polish.

1. **ONE authorized visual commit per navigation.** A click, route update, Promise resolution, cache hit, loading=false or 300ms timer is only intent, NOT authorization to reveal the destination. For another folder/album/collection, retain last accepted rows/crumbs/rail, stable scroll-host DOM identity, scrollTop, saved folder ID and Viewer return anchor until current Owner/session/lifecycle/request generation AND canonical authorized first counted Range or index receipt match the target. Reveal the new view and update scroll/storage ONCE in the pre-paint commit. FORBID beginBrowse/scrollTo(0)/virtualizer reset/clearing old rows or writing target storage in asynchronous acknowledgement callbacks before that receipt. Failed, canceled, stale, denied or superseded requests must leave the previous committed presentation unchanged. Same-folder activation may skip a redundant read only when that exact accepted destination is already verified.
2. **Never create an ephemeral intermediate UI.** FORBID ordinary folder/album/app/rail navigation screens, captions or spinner/skeleton rows such as “正在加载文件夹…”, “正在读取相册…”, or an inserted progress ring after a 300ms delay, even for genuinely slow GETs. Suppress only the decorative display, NEVER the real network request. A cold/unverified screen reserves a persistent silent slot, exposes truthful aria-busy, and must not show an invented error or zero. No blank→error→loading→content, accepted rows→spinner→rows, moving breadcrumb/header, or extra scroll host. Retry, actual terminal error and accepted data remain visible.
3. **Verified data, not Boolean busy, owns empty/error.** Track unverified / ready / error for current Owner + source + query/directory + generation. Initial empty array/null or loading=false BEFORE useEffect runs does NOT prove “暂无/空文件夹/还没有相册” or request failure. Only an accepted, current, authenticated ready-zero may display empty. Keep last verified rows or verified-empty on same-owner refresh; distinguish terminal first-request failure from unrequested state, preserve actionable retry, and never paint another Owner's cached data. Fence every late callback and cancellation.
4. **Geometry and scroll must be stable.** Preserve xDrive 52px global app header, one mounted Files/Gallery virtual scroll host, and existing Viewer anchor/focus. Cold, pending, accepted-empty and error states on the same surface MUST use identical reserved minimum geometry. Toolbar, Search, Sidebar, crumbs, pagination and loading indicators may not conditionally insert/remove rows or change height. FORBID height/maxHeight/padding/margin/top/list-layout animation in response to scrolling or short busy flags; keep 44px touch targets, keyboard focus and reduced-motion behavior. Do not add a second virtualizer, materialize 100k entries, or schedule another scroll-to-top effect.
5. **Explicit real-progress exceptions, NOT a spinner loophole.** Preserve real asynchronous thumbnail/file preview, Live Photo resource, video poster, upload/download/sync progress, user-invoked indexing, durable operations, Search/Trash, and legitimate page/viewport fetch feedback when genuinely useful. If shown, feedback must use a reserved non-reflowing slot and truthful state rather than a decorative navigation timer. Never disable AbortController, Go ctx cancellation, retry or error disclosure to mask a spinner. Do not blanket-disable all CSS transitions or accessibility cues.
6. **Mandatory tests and evidence on EVERY affected entry.** Update tests mounting the ORIGINAL React components plus a built Chromium browser navigation scenario (or explicitly justify an unavailable platform/entry). Force before-first-GET loading=false, fast and held >300ms response, accepted zero/nonzero, terminal failure/retry, out-of-order and rapidly superseded requests, owner switch, back/deep link, Viewer return, scroll. Assert ONE scroll host, same old visible anchor/title/saved ID during pending, exactly ONE reset only after success; verify header/crumb/scroll-host rectangles within 1 CSS px during held navigation. Never assert that an overscanned first DOM row is the user's visible anchor. Test Web wide, Mobile Web 375/390/899px, Desktop and installed PWA; report native iOS Safari/Android/orientation/keyboard/slow WAN as NOT RUN until physically checked. Continue the existing P58-P77 mounted/browser suites; extend new surfaces instead of only adding another static string test. See [desktop/tests/navigation-no-jitter-contract-p78.cjs](desktop/tests/navigation-no-jitter-contract-p78.cjs).
7. **Mandatory PR / release gate.** Every navigation, loading/readState, spinner/skeleton, route, geometry, scroll or Range change MUST document previous committed content, authenticated receipt boundary, before/held/after frames, failure/retry, and cross-platform effect. A newly appearing intermediate frame or unexplained layout movement BLOCKS merge. Require exact-HEAD full CI, actual relevant renderer + Chromium regression and Final Gate; distinguish source-tested, built-browser-tested, physically measured and merged. Do NOT invent CLS/FPS, claim iOS physical parity or mark release GO from desktop Chromium emulation. Keep independent Go Race, security, transfers and physical 100k performance benchmarks separately scoped.

**Maintainability:** This is evergreen policy, not a progress log. Keep Web/Mobile Web/PWA on the same Server API, shared state, virtualized Range and Gallery Viewer, with Desktop using shared UI/Agent transport. Do not change App Frame geometry to mimic iOS, and do not dilute the policy when a new page is added.

## PWA safe-delivery policy (2026-10-12)

The dedicated PWA audit and release gates are in [docs/pwa-delivery.md](docs/pwa-delivery.md); platform-specific S5 real-device go/no-go records are in [docs/pwa-s5-acceptance.md](docs/pwa-s5-acceptance.md). A Linux HTTPS fixture or mobile UA simulation is never evidence of physical iOS 27/Android/Desktop Installed acceptance. Preserve the 52px full-screen App Frame, the shared Web/Mobile Web Files/Gallery REST/controllers/ranges and existing durable Server tasks. Register only in a secure production Web context; never persist auth, API, thumbnails, original file bytes, downloads, Range or private Share responses in the public PWA Service Worker cache. Never auto-reload a running transfer/Viewer or claim iOS lock-screen background work. Treat installed-mode native-device acceptance separately from Manifest/source/Chromium tests. Keep PWA changes off unrelated performance/race/gallery branches.

## Push/Pull apps and device-local backup ownership (2026-10-10)

Read `docs/sync-folder-apps.md` and `docs/local-folder-push.md` before changing synchronization-folder UI, routes, Source handlers, device registration, or execution permissions.

- **Two first-level applications**, not two tabs or a parent navigation item: **设备备份** (Push) and **远程拉取** (Pull). Internal connector records remain **Source / 同步文件夹** for API/storage compatibility, but user-visible **远程拉取** screens must call them **拉取来源** (or simply 来源), not 同步文件夹; omit redundant Pull/Push direction badges in the Pull-only app. **设备备份** uses backup/device wording. Never rename persisted paths, Source IDs, or transports for a display-copy change. Keep the existing full-screen App Frame, 52px global title bar, unified shared MUI fundamentals, and shared Server `Source / SourceItem / SyncRun / Planner / CAS` contracts. Separate business controllers, creation flows, detail views, application routes and state; route legacy `sync-folders?source=ID` links by persisted `direction`, never by `kind` alone.
- **Push is device-local, including under the same signed-in account.** The Desktop on physical device A may create/configure/preview/start/pause/cancel/retry/remove *only sources whose locally authorized Root belongs to device A*. Desktop B may **view only** A's backups, just like Web/Mobile Web; never treat another user's login, the same account, a host name, Web origin, `User-Agent`, or caller-controlled device headers as local execution authority. An Agent's already-authorized background schedule may run without an open Desktop window. Never make closing a window a durable-sync cancel command.
- **Read-only visibility scope B:** for authorized owner devices show device name/platform, trusted heartbeat or explicitly unknown state, Source/folder display name, Server-resolved cloud target, backup policy, real progress and totals, and paginated run summaries/history. Remote/Desktop-other and Web/Mobile Web must not receive local absolute paths, Root fingerprints, device credentials, detailed local file listings, raw failure strings that reveal such paths, mutable Source contracts or operation actions. Use a deliberately redacted read DTO; do not rely on hiding an edit button.
- **Local-only mutation must be enforced by the Server and owning Agent**, before activating `local_folder`: prove possession of the bound device credential plus authorization for that exact Root and Source/revision at each mutating boundary. Owner JWT alone can authorize safe reads, not remote Source create/config/trigger/cancel/delete or binding mutation. A revoked or different device cannot execute stale work. Preserve old Source ID/Node/SourceItem/alias/history and Backups' two-complete-scans/24h/trash-only Mirror safety. Do not lift existing local Source activation/Run gates early.
- **Account-security exception:** a signed-in owner may remotely **revoke lost-device authentication** from an independent account-security control, with appropriate audit/owner scope. That action never starts/stops/changes a Push Source and is NOT a button in 设备备份. Revocation invalidates subsequent device-authorized operations; do not promise instant termination of a durable transfer without its own verified fencing/cancel contract.
- **NAS Push:** NAS remains its own local execution/configuration authority. This round builds **no new NAS agent, NAS pairing, remote operation, general-device binding or NAS Push manager**. The new 设备备份 UI reserves a clearly non-operational **待支持** NAS entry only. Preserve legacy NAS CLI-created Source records, committed bytes, histories and background operation; document any legacy owner-JWT remote-control endpoint as a transitional security gap rather than claiming enforced local-only NAS controls.
- **Pull:** Yike Photos, Synology Photos Pull and Synology File Station Pull remain Server-executed and fully manageable from Web/Desktop; preserve cookie/DSM secret contracts, schedules, scan/run/retry, current target binding, owners and existing transport. Classify `synology_photos` by **`kind + direction`**, because it exists in both families. Generic-source endpoints must reject disallowed local Push writes by device identity without breaking Pull.
- **Verification and handoff:** tests must cover A controlling A, Desktop B/Web viewing (including accurate progress and history) but failing every A write even if both Desktop installs share an account, forged headers/device IDs, revoked devices, unknown heartbeat, path/error redaction, account-security revocation only, old deep links, existing Source IDs, Pull full parity, and NAS placeholder/legacy data retention. Distinguish implemented, CI-tested, merged and physically measured states in every continuation report. Use the canonical phased checklist in `docs/sync-folder-apps.md`.

## Repository workflow

For every code change in this repository, use this workflow by default:

Provider delivery rules:

- **GitLab:** after the applicable local validation passes, make a normal commit on the latest GitLab `master` and push it directly to `gitlab/master`. Never force-push. Treat the resulting GitLab `master` pipeline as the authoritative post-push validation; if it fails, diagnose it and fix forward with another normally pushed commit.
- **GitHub:** never push changes directly to `origin/master`. Push an exactly-one-commit short-lived branch, open a PR targeting `master`, require the PR CI to pass, and merge it with linear history.
- **Changes for both providers:** start from a commit shared by both masters whenever possible, validate locally, push the commit directly to GitLab `master`, and push that same commit to a short-lived GitHub branch for a PR. Do not push it directly to GitHub `master`.

1. Run `git fetch origin` and `git fetch gitlab` first when both remotes exist.
2. Before creating or continuing a work branch, reconcile only branches that are materially related to the current task, dependency chain, or touched subsystem:
   - inspect related unmerged branches for their diff, PR/MR state, CI state, conflicts, and unfinished work;
   - continue a viable related branch instead of duplicating the same work;
   - delete related branches whose commits are already fully contained in `origin/master`, or that are abandoned, duplicate, or superseded;
   - keep a related branch only when there is a concrete reason it cannot yet be merged or deleted, and state that reason.
   Do **not** inspect, wait on, merge, rebase, or otherwise manage unrelated branches/PRs/MRs merely because they exist. Unrelated work may proceed independently unless it creates an actual merge conflict or changes a dependency required by the current task.
   Do not start a fresh implementation while an older viable half-finished branch for the same or directly related work is still unresolved.
3. After reconciling the current task's related branches, fix the work baseline immediately and do not let unrelated later `master` movement churn the branch. GitLab work may continue on a clean local `master` based on `gitlab/master`; GitHub work uses a new short-lived branch from the selected `origin/master` baseline when no related existing branch should be continued. Create or preserve the branch's single work commit immediately after selecting that baseline.
   - Every short-lived work branch must carry **exactly one work commit above its selected base at all times after the first commit is created**. Do not accumulate follow-up, fixup, or "temporary" commits with the intention of squashing them later.
   - After the first commit, fold every subsequent change into that same work commit with `git commit --amend` (and force-push only the non-`master` work branch when needed). Keep the branch single-commit throughout development, review fixes, and pre-merge reconciliation.
   - For closely related dependent phases, do **not** wait for an upstream PR/MR to merge before continuing. Pull/reuse the active work branch directly, or create a short-lived stacked branch from its current tested head when that is the fastest safe path.
   - A stacked/dependent branch must likewise add exactly one work commit above its immediate dependency branch. After the dependency lands, rebase/reconstruct that one commit onto the latest `origin/master`; never use a late squash as normal cleanup.
   - Do not block implementation merely because a related PR is waiting on CI/review when the dependency branch is available and its current tree is suitable for continued work.
4. Make the requested change only on the selected delivery branch, or on the clean local `master` selected for a GitLab direct push. Preserve the one-work-commit invariant on every short-lived GitHub branch.
5. Add or update relevant tests.
6. Run the applicable local tests and require them to pass before opening or updating the PR/MR.
7. Before the first delivery push, fetch the target remote again to detect real integration problems, but do not chase unrelated `master` movement after the work baseline has been fixed. For GitHub, keep the existing single work commit on its selected baseline when the PR remains mergeable; reconstruct that one commit onto the newer `origin/master` only when GitHub reports the PR cannot be merged because of an actual conflict, or when repository rules explicitly require an update. For GitLab, update the clean local `master` from `gitlab/master` before committing or pushing. Do not rebase merely because `master` has unrelated newer commits or merely to retrigger CI.
8. Before the first full GitHub PR validation, verify that the work branch is already exactly one commit relative to its merge base; do **not** plan a final squash after CI. For a normal branch based on `origin/master`, verify with:

   ```bash
   git rev-list --count origin/master..HEAD
   ```

   The result must be `1`. A result greater than `1` is a workflow violation to fix before full PR/MR validation, not an expected pre-merge cleanup step. The purpose of keeping the branch single-commit from the start is to avoid a late squash/force-push that causes an otherwise unnecessary additional CI run.
9. For GitHub, push the branch and open or update the PR; its CI is the authoritative pre-merge validation. For GitLab, push the locally validated commit directly to `gitlab/master`; its `master` pipeline is the authoritative post-push validation. Once the applicable pipeline is green, do not push, rebase, amend, squash, or otherwise retrigger CI unless the source tree must change.
10. If `origin/master` advances after CI is green, ignore unrelated commits and merge the already-tested PR directly when it remains mergeable. Reconstruct/rebase only when repository rules require it or an actual merge conflict makes the PR unmergeable. Any required reconstruction changes the tested commit and therefore requires the PR/MR CI to run again.
11. Merge an already-single-commit GitHub PR into `master` using a linear-history merge. GitLab changes are already on `master` through the validated normal push. Do not rely on squash merge to repair a multi-commit work branch.
   - For GitHub, the successful PR CI run is the merge gate. **After the PR merge succeeds, do not wait for, poll, or block subsequent work on the `master` push CI triggered by that merge.** Proceed immediately to cleanup and the next task.
   - Treat GitHub `master` CI as an asynchronous post-merge defense, not a second completion gate for the already-merged PR. If that later pipeline reports a regression attributable to the merged change, diagnose it and fix forward in a new normal PR; unrelated follow-on work must not wait for that pipeline to finish.
12. After the merge succeeds, delete the merged remote branch through the repository cleanup automation:
   - GitHub: `.github/workflows/cleanup-merged-branches.yml` runs on pushes to `master` and safely removes redundant merged branches, including rebase-merged PR branches whose original head SHA no longer appears in `master` history.
   - If an automatic cleanup run is missed or delayed, use that workflow's `workflow_dispatch` entry point instead of requiring a locally authenticated branch-delete command.
   - Verify the merged branch is gone after cleanup; do not leave superseded task branches around.
13. Keep long-lived branches to a minimum.

On failure, label the retained snapshot **stale** rather than presenting it as fresh. ## Service dependencies configuration and activation policy (2026-10-10)

- The administrator **系统依赖** page is the long-term management entry for **instance-wide** services, system-level provider credentials, model workers and deployment dependencies; Web/Desktop share the same MUI surface and Server-side admin authorization. Show every service's **configured/installed/health/activation** states separately. Display `config_mode`, `apply_mode` and an exact requirement, not a fake green status or an inert enable button.
- **Strict boundary:** exclude all per-user Yike, Synology Photos, DSM FileStation and **同步文件夹** source records, credentials and navigation from this administrator page, its status API and its configuration contracts. Users manage them only from their own **同步文件夹** surfaces, with their existing authorization rules. Sharing masked/reveal credential UI is allowed; sharing secret storage, API permissions or global source data is not.
- **Baidu Server API:** runtime-encrypted Server AK hot update/temporary audited reveal; new requests use the changed AK without restart. No alternate/offline map fallback.
- **Final acceptance is actual configuration and application for every instance-wide dependency:** an administrator must eventually be able to inspect current/effective configuration, validate a proposed revision, save it, apply it by the safe service-specific mode, verify runtime health and see failed/pending/rolled-back results. Saving desired state alone never counts as applied. Preserve revision/concurrency checks, audited changes and rollback. Until the backend truly supports a stage, show its exact missing prerequisite and disable its action rather than using an inert button.
- **Caddy AliDNS live read-only permission test P1-CD3b2b:** only an explicit admin action with exact saved HTTPS and credential revisions can issue one fixed signed `DescribeSubDomainRecords` read over HTTPS to `dns.aliyuncs.com`. Server validates both revisions, decrypts solely the global AliDNS keyring row, audits before outbound call, bounds timeout/response, rejects redirects/proxies and rate-limits. Never take domain, endpoint, path, method, DNS action, or credential from the renderer. Responses/audits must not expose keys, domain, record data, raw cloud errors or signed headers. Re-check revisions after the response. Only success of the actual authenticated **read** may say read-authorized, and it must still return write_permission_verified=false, apply_supported=false, public_https_verified=false. Failure/unreachable/unknown are not success. Does not issue DNS TXT writes, invoke Caddy/Host Manager/Docker, mutate Source/sync data or cancel durable transfers; real privileged host apply and DNS-01 edit permissions remain future work.
- **Caddy HTTPS local prerequisite preflight P1-CD3b2a:** the administrator-only, no-store read-only preflight combines saved desired enable/domain/revision, encrypted AliDNS pair integrity (only local AEAD decrypt, no plaintext response), and fresh allowlisted Host Manager Caddy health. It must return explicit seven-part fixed-state checks and distinguish enable, disable, and unset; a saved disable does not require AliDNS. It may say local configuration is complete without implying current host apply. Old/stale/invalid host observation is unknown, local TLS proof is only for the already-host-configured endpoint and never binds to desired revision, and public HTTPS/DNS/ACME remains unverified. **Always** keep apply_supported false with a blocked restricted-apply check until an independent audited no-lockout host transaction is delivered; no path/URL/command, arbitrary network input, DNS/ACME call, Docker, secret export, Host Manager request, Source mutation or durable transfer disruption is allowed in this phase.
- **Caddy AliDNS global credential staging P1-CD3b1:** use the existing Server keyring only to encrypt an atomic AliDNS AK ID / AK Secret pair under a unique AAD kind and ID, in a global admin-owned record. It is never part of user Source/同步文件夹 secret storage or public dependency status. Save/rotate/clear must require a revision and metadata-only atomic audit; explicit temporary reveal must be separately admin-authorized and audited. UI must conceal by default and on hidden/window/account changes. **Never** claim that a staged secret is deployed or valid: apply_supported stays false; no Caddy/DNS/ACME/Docker/Host Manager operation, deployment env edit, Source/sync mutation or transfer interruption occurs in this phase. P1-CD3b2+ must separately design restricted encrypted delivery, real credential preflight, no-lockout deployment, rollback, exact revision proof, and network/HTTPS verification before offering actual apply.\n- **Caddy HTTPS revisioned desired configuration P1-CD3a:** admins can edit *desired* enablement and a tightly validated ASCII FQDN (strict label lengths, no IP literals, URLs, flags, wildcards, paths, ports or credentials) in the shared Web/Desktop MUI service page. The Server enforces administrator authentication, optimistic database revision fencing, immutable history including default revision 0, metadata-only transactional audit, and rollback by a new revision. This is NOT a Caddy live-configuration controller: saving/rolling back must never touch the operator's `XD_DOMAIN`, AliDNS credentials, TLS certificates, Docker, reverse proxy, Host Manager work queue, upload/download/sync or media tasks. Host observation reports only independently established container health, HTTPS deployment-intent boolean and local TLS certificate state; these observations do not prove that the stored desired *domain and revision* were applied, so the admin API never returns `applied` and `apply_supported` stays false. No Caddy apply or container enable/disable HTTP/IPC action may exist until a future separately audited, allowlisted, lockout-safe host transaction supports validation, trusted reversible preflight, exact revision fencing, secure DNS secret handling and actual independent post-apply checks. Only after that may a saved desired revision be promoted to effective. No per-user 同步文件夹, Source/connector secrets or arbitrary Docker authority in this contract.
- **Caddy localhost HTTPS effect proof P1-CD2:** only the trusted Host Manager may validate a **host-configured ASCII FQDN and numeric HTTPS port**; it must reject flags, host paths, arbitrary URLs, malformed domains and invalid ports before any probe. Connect only to the fixed host loopback address (127.0.0.1) with a short timeout; use SNI and OpenSSL certificate trust-chain plus exact hostname verification, never DNS-resolve or HTTP-fetch user input. Do not reveal the domain, certificate, AliDNS credential, port binding, raw OpenSSL output, logs or Docker socket to Server/Web/Desktop. Publish a small allowlisted `tls_state` and capability flag alongside Caddy's fixed-container Docker health with fresh Host Manager heartbeat and bounded regular-file validation. Old Host Managers must not claim HTTPS ready; healthy HTTP with failed HTTPS is unavailable, TLS unverifiable is unknown, a validated loopback handshake can be locally ready **only while explicitly saying public HTTPS/DNS/firewall/WAN reachability is unverified**. Web/Desktop share a read-only Caddy configured/observed TLS status. No Caddy restart, arbitrary control, live domain edit or enabling/disabling from the admin page until a separate audited, reversible, lockout-safe maintenance contract exists.
- **Caddy fixed gateway health P1-CD1:** the administrator dependency list may consume only a bounded, read-only health observation for fixed `xdrive-caddy` published by the existing Host Manager. The Server cannot invoke Docker or accept a path/URL/command from Web/Desktop. The runner records an allowlisted Docker running/health state and a boolean showing only whether the host environment *requests* `XD_DOMAIN`; no domain, AliDNS secret, logs, certificate material, host path or container ID enters the status API. The Server checks fresh heartbeat, regular size-limited status file, age/future-skew bounds and allowlisted states. Only a genuine healthy Caddy Docker healthcheck may show **internal HTTP ingress** as ready. This does not prove public HTTPS certificate validity, DNS, ACME issuance, TLS handshake or WAN reachability; configured domain is intent, not applied TLS. Stopped/unhealthy -> unavailable; old/missing/stale/invalid/starting/unverified -> unknown. Do not offer a Caddy enable/disable button or domain editor until a separate auditable, reversible maintenance+TLS-validation contract avoids administrator lockout and interruption of file transfers.
- **GeoNames P1-B2a:** Match radius is an administrator-only, revision-guarded persisted setting; build and verify the complete immutable dataset at the chosen radius before writing setting+audit atomically and swapping the current Server instance. At startup saved radius overrides deployment defaults. Report desired and this instance's effective radius separately; never claim all replicas are applied. Dataset path, cluster coordination and rollback remain separate. No user Sync Folder configuration is exposed.
- **GeoNames P1-B1 prerequisite:** A mounted dataset may be validated and manually hot-reloaded only through an admin-authorized version-guarded, audited endpoint. Rebuild the immutable resolver completely before swapping; PlaceRunner must pin one snapshot for each batch. Never accept an arbitrary host filesystem path from Web/Desktop or claim multi-replica activation. Dataset directory changes, coordinated apply and rollback remain unimplemented; matching-distance persistence is tracked in P1-B2a.
- **GeoNames, Photo Intelligence, Worker, Caddy, PostgreSQL, file storage and future Media Worker:** backend configuration/control must be implemented and tested per dependency before advertising an edit/apply action. Clearly mark deployment-only settings as requiring restricted maintenance, controlled restart or task boundary. Never expose a Docker socket or restart core services merely because a user saved text; no interrupted durable upload/download/sync as a side effect.
- **P1-PH1b1 restricted Photo Intelligence host enable (2026-10-12):** Begin real deployment control only with the single fixed optional `photo-intelligence` Profile and fixed `photo-face` service. The Host Manager (never the Server Docker socket) may process a bounded, fresh, revision-fenced enable-only request. If the optional container is already healthy, acknowledge without restart; if it is **provably stopped**, start only `photo-face` with `--no-deps` and require fixed Docker health, never `--force-recreate`, broad Compose up/down or restart. Refuse unknown/unhealthy/running-unverified hosts and all **disable requests** until an independent cross-replica active photo-work drain/lease fence exists. Persist/recover only the exact COMPOSE_PROFILES token while preserving every other Profile/env value; an interrupted/failed transaction reports failed, restores previous Profile and can stop only the target newly started by that transaction. The trusted Host Manager may advertise an observed applied revision, but PH1b1 alone does **not** constitute an admin-authorized Server apply: no public `applied` state or UI start/stop until the next separately audited Server request bridge and desired revision/token proof, including cross-instance fencing, is implemented. Keep all user 同步文件夹 and durable transfers untouched. Add fake-Docker shell tests in GitHub and GitLab validation.
- **Photo Intelligence shared host desired revision P1-PH1a (2026-10-12):** the four fixed analyzer rows share one optional `photo-intelligence` deployment Profile and one fixed `xdrive-photo-face` container observation, not four independently switchable containers. Only `photo-face` exposes the common host **desired enable** editor and immutable history/rollback; other model rows link to it. Persist `AdminPhotoHostSetting` and history under a dedicated administrator-owned namespace, with optimistic CAS, metadata-only atomic audit, bounded revision/history, explicit admin authentication and old-Agent unsupported fallback; leave the separate `photo-auto` task-admission policy untouched. Revision zero is **unmanaged**, not an observed disabled host. A saved or rolled-back host desire **never applies** the deployment: `apply_supported=false`, `apply_state=pending` and no POST apply endpoint, Docker command, Host Manager request, Compose write, user Source/同步文件夹 write or transfer/job cancellation. Even a fresh healthy container with matching Profile boolean has no desired-revision acknowledgment, so it is still pending. Expose only a fixed, fresh host observation with no secrets/images/paths; no first-visit global service-list credential/history fetch. Follow-on P1-PH1b needs a separate allowlisted, audited, no-durable-work-interruption Host Manager transaction with exact revision fencing, opt-in Profile reconciliation, safe drain, rollback and independent effect/model probes before enabling the actual apply button. Full exact-head CI and physical opt-in deployment are separate gates.
- **Photo Intelligence enable preflight P1-PH1b2a:** The shared System Dependencies photo-host `检测` pane may explicitly request a no-store **read-only** fixed 7-check local preflight from an admin-only endpoint. Inspect only saved desired revision, two trusted fresh fixed-host observations, and no-follow existence of fixed queue/active/recovery files. Uncertain/old host or blocked queue cannot pass. The preflight's local_checks_complete is never permission to apply: admin-dispatch stays blocked, effect-proof stays unknown, `apply_supported=false`, and no POST apply route may be introduced before audited, cross-replica revision/token fencing and independent model health proof. Desired disable always blocked until real cross-instance work drain. No Docker/Host Manager mutation, user Source access, transfer cancellation or unsafe cross-app preflight. Shared Web/Mobile Web/Desktop must offer identical manually-triggered detail-only detection; clear short-lived proof when detail or revision changes. See docs/service-dependencies.md#p1-ph1b2a-2026-10-12-photo-intelligence-enable-preflight-no-dispatch.
- **Photo Intelligence audited enable dispatch P1-PH1b2b (2026-10-12):** A new administrator-only `POST /services/photo-intelligence/host/apply` may dispatch ONLY an audited, saved **enabled=true** revision to the fixed `photo-intelligence` / `photo-face` Host Manager. Use PostgreSQL's saved desired row `FOR UPDATE` as a cross-Server serialization lock and a persistent one-slot `AdminPhotoHostApply` gate with immutable audit authorization before atomic, no-clobber request publication. Reject stale/disable/replay/unknown/unhealthy/busy host state, and fence desired save/rollback across replicas while a request is reserved or queued. Never automatically drop an ambiguous reservation, recover a stale request by time alone, or claim it was never executed after a filesystem failure. Only an exact fresh fixed-host request-ID/revision/healthy/Profile receipt with no pending/active/recovery marker closes the gate; verified host start still does NOT prove model `Info`, fleet-wide application or automatic photo-admission. Display `queued`, `failed`, and `host-verified` separately; no fully-applied status without an independent model probe. A failed request can be superseded only after trusted fixed-host evidence confirms safe completion and no pending artifacts. Preserve `photo-auto`, four independent analyzer probes, all user Source/同步文件夹, Caddy/core, transfer/sync processes and the 52px shared Web/Desktop/Mobile Web App Frame. Disable/stop/restart stay unsupported until cross-instance photo-work draining and recovery. See [docs/service-dependencies.md](docs/service-dependencies.md#p1-ph1b2b-2026-10-12-audited-enable-only-cross-replica-host-dispatch).
- **Media Worker PID budget P1-M1c2c (supersedes earlier PID deployment-only status):** add only the isolated FFmpeg Media Worker PID count, 64–1024 in increments of 32 (default 128), into the existing administrator-owned `enabled`/`memory_mib`/`cpu_milli` CAS revision, immutable history and audited rollback. Apply remains a separate audited request to the fixed, online, PID-capable Host Manager; the Server must never invoke Docker. The Host Manager may update only the media-worker Compose profile and fixed memory/CPU/PID environment scalars without modifying other settings or containers. Enabled application requires matching actual Docker `HostConfig.Memory`, `HostConfig.NanoCpus`, `HostConfig.PidsLimit` and real FFmpeg/FFprobe probes, while disabled applies only after stopped state plus verified staged limits. Any old runner, pending operation, revision mismatch, cgroup drift, missing PID observation, failed apply or no heartbeat must not show `applied`. Rollback of host configuration and optional Worker state is best effort, failure must remain visible; no Server/Caddy/PostgreSQL/Pull Worker restart, per-user 同步文件夹 data or durable transfer interruption. This does not deliver real transcoding/media jobs, queueing, cancellation, multi-replica controls or Rootless opt-in physical verification.
- **Media Worker CPU quota P1-M1c2b:** an administrator may save a bounded 250–4000 mCPU integer (step 250; default 750) together with enabled and memory_mib as one CAS-guarded, audited, immutable-history revision. No saved revision is applied by itself. Only a CPU-capable fresh Host Manager may accept an audited apply tuple of fixed request ID, revision, boolean, memory_mib and cpu_milli. It atomically updates only media-worker COMPOSE_PROFILES, XD_MEDIA_WORKER_MEMORY_LIMIT and XD_MEDIA_WORKER_CPU_LIMIT, preserving unrelated deployment settings. For enabled apply, require actual Docker HostConfig.Memory bytes and HostConfig.NanoCpus equals cpu_milli * 1000000, plus genuine FFmpeg/FFprobe health. Disabled apply observes a stopped process and correct staged resource values, not a live cgroup. Old hosts, drift, pending requests, failures and absent verification must not display applied. Recover by restoring prior optional Worker settings/state only, never restart the core, DB, Pull Worker, Caddy, Source/sync processes or durable transfers. PID and media-job APIs, real Rootless opt-in, and fleet-wide application remain future phases.
- **Media Worker memory budget P1-M1c2a:** global administrator-owned FFmpeg Media Worker memory limit is a bounded CAS revisioned integer MiB setting (256–4096 MiB, divisible by 128; previous deployment default 768 MiB). It is stored together with enabled/disabled desired state, audited and retained in immutable history/rollback. The separate, audited Host Manager apply request contains only ID/revision/boolean/memory integer; never accept arbitrary env variable names, Docker options, host paths, image, CPU/PID tunables or Source data. Host Manager atomically preserves unrelated Compose settings while updating only the media-worker profile and `XD_MEDIA_WORKER_MEMORY_LIMIT`, recreates **only media-worker** for enabled apply, validates actual `docker inspect --format '{{.HostConfig.Memory}}' xdrive-media-worker` in bytes and FFmpeg/FFprobe Unix health, or verifies the staged limit and stopped state for disabled apply. On failure restore previous config and best-effort previous optional-worker state; do not claim successful or global activation. Live drift, old runners and missing memory proof MUST remain failed/pending. The Server never gains Docker authority; no core Server/Caddy/PostgreSQL/Pull Worker restarts, no durable transfer cancellation. CPU/PID configuration, job protocol and physical Rootless opt-in remain separate unimplemented stages.
- **Media Worker restricted administrator activation P1-M1c1:** the admin may persist and audit a global desired boolean activation revision, inspect bounded immutable history, roll back by creating a new revision, and explicitly apply via a separate audited Host Manager authorization. The Server never invokes Docker or accepts a service name, shell command, image, file path, restart target, JWT, secret, Source or media bytes from the renderer. The host runner recognizes only a fixed `media-worker-control-request.json` tuple (24 lowercase hex request ID, positive bounded revision, boolean enabled, fresh UTC timestamp); serializes with existing server update execution, fences stale applied revisions and permits explicit same-revision recovery only after failed/drift status. It uses allowlisted Docker Compose `up -d --no-deps media-worker`, `exec media-worker ... check`, and `stop media-worker` only; writes COMPOSE_PROFILES atomically while retaining unrelated profiles, and never restarts core Server, worker Pull, DB, storage or Caddy. A request cannot be queued before durable authorization audit; saving desired alone does not apply. Effective status requires matching desired/host revision, observed real container state, and live FFmpeg/FFprobe probe when enabled; a down Host Manager, missing heartbeat, queued/in-progress/stale result, failed action or mismatched revision must NOT show applied. A pending/active request fences new desired saves even across runner outages. Shared Web/Desktop UI uses the same admin API and MUI panel with explicit confirm, desired/apply separation, safe history and manual verification; no cross-replica fleet success or media-job readiness claim. Operator-owned paths and Rootless optional-profile acceptance remain separately verified.
- **Media Worker deployable runtime P1-M1b:** package the *same source* as a separate nonroot Alpine image with actual FFmpeg/FFprobe and a default-off `COMPOSE_PROFILES=media-worker` service. Worker has network_mode none, read-only root, no data/DB/credential mounts, private operator-provisioned Unix socket volume and hard CPU/RAM/PID caps; Server mounts this socket directory read-only. The Server image stays distroless. Installer opt-in must pull the exact separately CI-produced and published image, start the profile, verify real Unix probe before reporting success and retain its existing rollback safety; both GitHub/GitLab image manifests must include this artifact. The admin health page can truthfully show disabled/unavailable/real FFmpeg runtime ready, **never** media task execution, and still cannot start/stop containers from Server. Subsequent phases need signed-byte job API, bounded queue, cancellation and audited admin control with separately authorized Host Manager apply/reconciliation. No renderer Docker authority or user Sync Folder data.
- **Media Worker real subprocess readiness P1-M1a:** only operator-provisioned absolute Unix sockets, in private directories and mode-0600, may expose an isolated FFmpeg runtime to Server. Versioned read-only probes must actually execute the fixed ffmpeg/ffprobe -version commands within time/output bounds, rejecting malformed protocol/redirects, without accepting user URLs, paths, flags, bytes, Docker actions or Source data. Admin states: disabled without socket, unavailable on failed probe, ready strictly for executable runtime health; even healthy probes never claim a working media task queue. The operator owns deployment/restart and path provisioning. No fake admin enable/config controls or interference with durable uploads/downloads/sync.
- **GeoNames missing-archive recovery P1-B3d1:** administrators may restore only the **currently desired** content-addressed GeoNames archive when its directory is completely absent from this Server's operator-provisioned private persistent snapshot directory. Input is restricted to the desired revision and current effective version/fingerprint; no arbitrary target, host path, bytes or network/replica fetch. The fixed trusted read-only deployment mount must revalidate all three bounded regular files to the **existing immutable manifest's exact SHA-256 content address and total size**, with a complete resolver rebuilt before publication. Existence of any target entry (including corrupt/symlink) MUST prohibit overwrite/deletion and instead report a controlled-maintenance requirement. Restore uses an admin-only audited, revision-locked action; on audit failure remove only the newly published directory and preserve last-good runtime/desired revision. On success hot-swap just this instance and acknowledge the **existing** saved revision; do not claim fleet recovery, remote data transport, general corrupt-directory repair or durable uploads. Status only observes directory existence, never pretends to have freshly hashed a present snapshot.
- **PostgreSQL xDrive Server pool tuning P1-PG1:** the administrator may modify only this application's `database/sql` maximum open and maximum idle connection counts (max-open 0=Go default unlimited or 8–256; max-idle 0–32 and <= nonzero max-open). A complete revision-guarded request, immutable revision history and metadata-only audit must commit before the responding Server calls thread-safe pool setters. On startup each Server reads saved desired settings, applies to its *own* DB handle and records the current-process effective revision; other replicas reconcile every 30 seconds without a fleet-success claim. A save failure/invalid value/missing audit never changes the pool; an applied claim needs observed `sql.DB.Stats().MaxOpenConnections` and matching local policy. Saving a revision can change queueing latency but must never restart the core, mutate PostgreSQL server connections/DSN/credentials, manipulate Docker/mounts, cancel existing transfers or manage per-user Source/同步文件夹. Default/unmanaged has no falsely asserted effective version; the database readiness probe remains separate. Historical rollback creates a new audited revision, not an in-place history rewrite. Operator-owned PostgreSQL settings, version upgrades, backups, server resource limits and storage migration remain deployment-only.
- **PostgreSQL observed-instance pool verification P1-PG2:** each xDrive Server publishes only a private, short-lived lease with the observed local database/sql max-open limit and actually installed max-idle policy/revision. Heartbeat interval 5 seconds, TTL 20 seconds, bounded 100+1 admin-only read, no instance ID/path/DSN/credential in response. The shared Web/Desktop page must distinguish current-process desired/effective from aggregated *observed-live* replica state. A revision is acknowledged only by its own process after setter completion and Stats().MaxOpenConnections verification. Missing/expired/invalid leases, zero observed rows, truncated scans or DB errors never become a cluster-wide success; even all observed rows matching does not prove every provisioned Server is online. Unmanaged defaults never count as managed applied. No container action, deployment writes, per-user Source data or durable-transfer interruption.
- **GeoNames persisted dataset selection P1-B3c:** An administrator-selected GeoNames content address (empty for the deployment mount) is saved atomically with a single settings revision, immutable radius history and metadata-only audit before the responding Server swaps a verified immutable resolver. Startup and each replica's passive reconciliation must rehash every locally archived input before acknowledging the desired revision. A missing/corrupt archive leaves last-good or an explicitly unacknowledged deployment fallback, never a fabricated applied/fleet state. Radius updates, history rollback and manual reload retain the desired selected source; other instances may remain pending until their own independently provisioned archives validate. No user Source data, renderer host path, raw dataset bytes, Docker socket, or core restart.
- **GeoNames instance-local data-file apply P1-B3b:** a local archived version may only be loaded after re-hashing the exact bounded, fixed GeoNames input files and reconstructing a verified immutable resolver. Only accept a journaled SHA-256 fingerprint or the literal deployment source, and fence expected current version/fingerprint and radius revision. Commit metadata-only audit before the atomic resolver pointer swap; preserve in-flight pinned snapshots and the last-good runtime on any error. Further radius updates must revalidate the chosen archived data, not silently fall back to deployment. UI must say **current-instance temporary apply, lost at Server restart**; this is not cluster coordination, automatic restore or dataset upload. Never expose host paths or Docker authority.\n- **GeoNames immutable dataset staging P1-B3a:** Only copy the three fixed, regular, size-bounded files from the trusted read-only deployment mount to an operator-provisioned independent persistent directory; never accept host paths or raw dataset bytes from a renderer in this stage. Copy to private temp, hash and fully validate the immutable resolver, then record an audited content-addressed manifest. Staged is explicitly **not applied** and only local directory presence can be observed; do not promise a fleet restore. Stale radius or resolver versions, malformed/partial datasets, audit failures and duplicate fingerprints must preserve the active resolver and existing archived versions. No Docker socket or user Sync Folder data.\n- **GeoNames multi-instance observation:** if aggregating Server replica state, acknowledge an audited radius revision only after the immutable resolver is effective on that instance; report exact revision, matching radius and content fingerprint from expiring process leases. Display only aggregate counts/fingerprint groups. Never expose replica IDs/hostnames/paths or claim a complete fleet inventory from observed-live heartbeats; zero rows, truncated results, mixed snapshots, invalid data or DB failures must never be presented as fully applied. Dataset file staging/distribution and rollback remain separate.
- Only mark **ready** after a genuine live probe; no probe means **unknown**, and unfinished integration means **planned**. Preserve authoritative statuses, admin audit, rollback path and actual-versus-desired configuration. Each phase adds tests; do not claim all providers fully managed until their real effect is proven.

## Race and concurrency specialization policy

- **Mandatory Race registry and de-duplication:** Before any Race/concurrency investigation, read [`docs/race-test-registry.md`](docs/race-test-registry.md) and compare the exact subsystem, competing operations, forced interleaving, identity/lifecycle and platform. Do not repeat accepted unchanged scenarios merely to restart a new chat. New source changes, new orderings/platforms, regressions and mandatory exact-head CI are legitimate repeat reasons. In the same one-work-commit PR, record every new meaningful probe and its authentic test-only First-red, rejected/invalid harness failures, unchanged reproducer, repaired exact-head CI and merge/branch disposition in that registry. Never claim an unrun test, physical E2E or GitLab validation.

- For a race/concurrency specialization task, **test first and do not change production behavior until a real failure is reproduced**. Continue running or extending the relevant race/stress/interleaving tests until one of them fails because of an actual product concurrency/lifecycle bug.
- A test harness, fixture, mock, parser, selector, timing assumption, CI infrastructure issue, or unrelated existing failure does **not** count as the required reproduction. Fix the test/harness itself and rerun without changing production code.
- The first-red evidence must be deterministic enough to identify the violated contract (for example: stale completion overwrites a newer scope, old lifecycle retains busy ownership, duplicate same-tick submission, old request crosses account/session identity, or a newer revision is regressed). Record that failing assertion/path in the PR description or test name.
- If no real race failure can be reproduced, keep testing/auditing; do not land speculative synchronization, generation fences, locks, cancellation, or lifecycle resets merely because a race appears theoretically possible.
- Only after a real first-red is confirmed may production code be changed. Make the smallest fix that addresses the reproduced ordering/lifecycle failure, and preserve existing platform semantics unless the failing contract proves they are wrong.
- After the fix, require the **same reproducer** to turn green, then run the materially related race/concurrency suite and normal subsystem tests. A replacement test that avoids the original failing interleaving is not sufficient.
- Race/concurrency branches follow the normal related-work rule: inspect and continue viable related branches/PRs first, avoid duplicate fixes, and keep the GitHub branch at exactly one work commit. Test-first revisions and production fixes must be amended into that same work commit rather than accumulated as fixup commits.
- Merge only after the reproduced race is fixed, the relevant regression suite is green, and the repository's normal authoritative CI gate passes. Do not merge a branch that is only statically plausible, partially tested, or still failing the original race.
- This policy applies equally to FileExplorer, Gallery/Photo UI, transfers, thumbnails, background tasks, sync, navigation/session lifecycle, caches, and any other subsystem where the task is specifically a race/concurrency investigation.

## Performance specialization policy

- **SH-P004 true 100k physical JPEG public-share original n3 measured (2026-10-18):** original source `9737639fcf068b51a9d00cc4e0064a1522ff98b6`, [real PG17/Gin/LocalCAS source CI #39126802572](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/39126802572) executed 1k smoke and then **3/3 genuine 100000 SHA-distinct, independently decodable JPEG physical CAS** samples, each +1 outside original, 1000 real public HTTP pages and three signed original stream SHA/decoder checks. Each Go test's `passed=true`; complete walk **3245.464/3224.434/3090.569ms (P50 3224.434ms)**, page P95 **3.814/3.876/3.705ms** and worst page **6.554ms**, signed ticket worst **5.634ms**, original signed stream worst **3.560ms**, Go sampled peak RSS delta max **622592B**; all frozen production gates PASS => unchanged production, `BEFORE=CURRENT`, AFTER/speedup N/A. Physically allocated source **409604096B** per fixture, 100000 original JPEG file bytes **70893376B**, seed **excluded**, all signed/denial/revoke original SHA valid. The workflow itself finished **RED only in the AFTER-GO Python summary** `KeyError 'original_source_bytes'` (actual source JSON field `physical_source_bytes`) so this run is **not** a valid green CI/final gate; retain failure and [raw artifact #12136304262](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/39126802572/artifacts/12136304262) and [permanent exact machine samples](docs/performance-evidence/sh-p004-physical-public-jpeg-100k/README.md), correct ONLY the summary dictionary lookup, then re-run whole exact-new-HEAD dedicated n3 and complete CI/FinalGate + GitHub clean mergeability before Ready/Rebase. One fixed-parent work commit, don't touch Race/durable jobs/production Go, data/frozen budgets or independent PRs. Not 100k simultaneous browser thumbnails, video/Live Motion, actual native/PWA/mobile GUI or WAN.

- **SH-P004 new authentic 100k physically distinct JPEG shared-folder original baseline (2026-10-18):** SH-P003 [#2070](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/pull/2070) merged, 100k PG metadata ONLY and 0 physical originals (real Browser n3 and accurate-head full 77-job CI green); do not retest it. Distinct SH-P004 opt-in source-executed Go HTTP to PostgreSQL17/LocalCAS must seed 100000 independently encoded, SHA-distinct decodable 80x64 JPEG originals (+ protected outside object), authenticate Owner's folder capability, truly read all 1000 public keyset pages, sign/download first/middle/last original bytes and verify exact SHA/JPEG decode, foreign/outside denied and revoke ticket. Freeze first-read gates BEFORE real run: 3 independent real physical sources, complete 100k walk<=90s, per-page p95<=500ms/worst<=2s, signed-ticket/stream each<=3s, Go sampled RSS growth<=256MiB; report CPU/RSS/HTTP bytes, physical allocated CAS, seed excluded. CURRENT Not measured until valid exact-head CI, AFTER/speedup N/A. Keep original production if within budget; real RED requires authentic same-runner matched >=3 BEFORE/AFTER and material gain. No Race, durable task cancellation, production endpoint, thumbnail rewrite or CAS architecture modifications. This is Server real TCP HTTP, not Web/Desktop GUI, PWA, WAN or 100k simultaneous thumbnail decode/Video/Live playback.   - **P056 第一次真实100k生产原版 RED（2026-10-17）：** 已纠正1k语义夹具后，准确 HEAD `1967aa8c7615e7617678ea5b96d40f0e18d589c9` 的[专项CI #38995429562](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/38995429562) 1k物理 smoke 成功（Owner Copy/Undo、授权永久删除和物理 SHA/RefCount 全过）。但第一组真正100k SHA不同原片在 Copy Worker 处理约第 12,853 个不同文件时失败：PostgreSQL17 `ERROR: out of shared memory (SQLSTATE 53200)`、`HINT: max_locks_per_transaction`（原日志约第419–420行，PG第496行）；Source 真实、操作有效但结果失败，故原版100k **0/1通过，不存在合法成功P50**，这是真正生产规模 RED 而非夹具问题。源码定位 `retainExistingContentReferenceTx` 每个SHA获取一直持续到同一十万文件复制事务末尾的独立 `pg_advisory_xact_lock`；新最小生产候选**只在现存、Ready且经源文件引用固定的Blob增引用路径**保留 `SELECT FOR UPDATE` 和原子 `ref_count+1` 行锁，消除随SHA数增加的事务 advisory locks，并对非Ready/缺失/尺寸不符拒绝。新建/Promote和最终GC的hash advisory锁不改，Undo/Trash/跨Owner权限和原始真实bytes照旧；以相同源规模真实3×100k、峰RSS/CPU/Go PID写盘、全部物理SHA/GC和全CI验收，未取得合格 AFTER 前不宣称提升，也不触碰Race专题。
  - **P056 真实1k预检 Invalid harness（2026-10-17）：** [专项 CI #38994723392](https://github.com/lazyxu/xdrive/actions/runs/38994723392) 在准确旧 HEAD `0636557455ec57a3f0d29ca10b589ba6ed11729e` 上通过 Go 编译/格式门禁，真实 PostgreSQL17、Owner HTTP Copy/Undo 和两个 Worker 均成功，但夹具错误断言 Undo 立即释放 copied CAS 引用，结果仅哨兵 RefCount1，1000 copied 原片仍 RefCount2。源码确认 Undo 是软删除 Trash，必须保留 Redo/Restore 数据，不能修改生产 RefCount 迎合夹具。修正为 Copy 1→2、Undo/Trash 仍2、Owner 带 If-Match 的授权永久删除才 2→1，单独计时（≤900s），并完整核验跨 Owner 404、实际物理 st_blocks、100001份SHA。原准确旧 HEAD **100k有效 n=0**，非生产性能 RED；新 HEAD 必须重新真实1k/3×100k和完整 CI/final-gate，生产/Race/耐久取消均不改。

All full-source SHA, authenticated Owner/privacy, allocated physical bytes, and final GC pass; all budgets met => **accept unchanged production / AFTER=N/A**. Archived original eight numeric/resource/source files + full eleven-file SHA manifest are linked from [CAS-A02c evidence](docs/performance-evidence/cas-a02c-real-http-owner-reuse-4gib/README.md). The archive changes work HEAD, so do not claim delivery/merge until fresh exact final head native n3 AND full 76-job CI incl. final-gate pass. This specific two-Owner workflow's six client modes remain Not measured; do not confuse with CAS-A02a first-upload 2× stage/write or CAS-A03-d3 B 1MiB changed/version full4GiB.

This is a genuinely unmeasured production Gin/PostgreSQL17/Local CAS layer: 3 independent 4GiB SHA-distinct AES-CTR sources, real Owner A 512×8MiB PUT, Owner A instant second File with 0 PUT, Owner B forbidden instant and required 512×8MiB PUT, one unchanged physical CAS after 3 references, 25ms RSS/CPU/actual staged st_blocks/Go write_bytes, verified full SHA and last Owner GC. Freeze original n>=3 latency/CPU/peak RSS/Go process write_bytes, actual st_blocks/inode count and full 100k bytes/SHA, rev/Owner checks, cross-owner protected data and zero duplicate CAS before proposing any product optimization. Seed and post-timer 100k SHA audit are excluded from HTTP latency; Linux Go PID writes exclude independent PG storage writes. Source-exact n>=3 must prove 511 server-reused source parts / 1 real changed PUT, scan/hash work, B full immutable CAS assembly+kernel write amplification, two historical/current full-Blob SHA/RefCounts, authenticated last-reference GC and full restoration. Freeze original budgets, raw artifact, [registry](docs/performance-test-registry.md) and [six-platform matrix](docs/performance-platform-coverage.md); benchmark-only proof is NOT production delta storage or six real clients. No Race changes or view-driven cancellation of durable user tasks.


- **CAS-A07 realistic 100k mixed-size Blob packing threshold:** consult [CAS-A07 physical mixed-size CAS/Pack performance](docs/cas-mixed-pack-performance.md) after [CAS-A05](docs/cas-small-pack-performance.md) and [CAS-A06](docs/cas-small-pack-cold-performance.md), and compare only a genuinely different source distribution. Freeze 95k 1KiB, 4k 16KiB, 900 128KiB, 100 1MiB unique true physical objects; use same-run immutable Local.Put/Open/Delete vs isolated binary-index Pack and measure real st_blocks, /proc/self/io, CPU/RSS, 2k true SHA/ReadAt, 95k GC/5k full survivors, mixed-size threshold, corruption rejection, and temporary Pack1+Pack2 peak. Even accepted offline Pack never proves production SQL Owner/RefCount/restore/FUSE/HTTP/WAN or six client modes, and never warrants changes to Race or user-owned durable task cancellation.
